# FIX AutoTest 测试用例详解

> 基于 YAML 用例自动生成 — CaseNo 精确匹配 + 多回报支持

## 用例总览

| 用例 | Steps | 多回报 |
|------|:---:|:---:|
| [Hy&BDQry-00010-2.yaml](#Hy&BDQry-00010-2yaml) | 4 | 2 |
| [MassQuote.yaml](#MassQuoteyaml) | 9 | 6 |
| [Position-00010-1.yaml](#Position-00010-1yaml) | 14 | 0 |
| [PositionMaintenance-00010-3.yaml](#PositionMaintenance-00010-3yaml) | 16 | 8 |
| [TAS-00001-4.yaml](#TAS-00001-4yaml) | 7 | 6 |
| [TAS-00001-6.yaml](#TAS-00001-6yaml) | 11 | 10 |
| [TAS-00002-5.yaml](#TAS-00002-5yaml) | 6 | 6 |
| [TJOrder-00003.yaml](#TJOrder-00003yaml) | 30 | 15 |
| [orderCancelReplace-00010-4.yaml](#orderCancelReplace-00010-4yaml) | 42 | 36 |
| [orderinsert-00001-1.yaml](#orderinsert-00001-1yaml) | 7 | 6 |
| [orderinsert-00001-3.yaml](#orderinsert-00001-3yaml) | 26 | 15 |
| [orderinsert-00002-2.yaml](#orderinsert-00002-2yaml) | 6 | 6 |

## Excel → YAML 对应关系

| Excel (.xlsx) | YAML (.yaml) | Steps | 多回报 | INI |
|--------------|-------------|:---:|:---:|------|
| orderinsert-00001-1.xlsx | orderinsert-00001-1.yaml | 7 | 6 | fix42-01 |
| orderinsert-00001-3.xlsx | orderinsert-00001-3.yaml | 26 | 15 | fix42-01 |
| orderinsert-00002-2.xlsx | orderinsert-00002-2.yaml | 6 | 6 | fix42-02 |
| MassQuote.xlsx | MassQuote.yaml | 9 | 6 | fix42-01 |
| TJOrder-00003.xlsx | TJOrder-00003.yaml | 30 | 15 | fix42-03 |
| Position-00010-1.xlsx | Position-00010-1.yaml | 14 | 0 | fix42-10 |
| PositionMaintenance-00010-3.xlsx | PositionMaintenance-00010-3.yaml | 16 | 8 | fix42-10 |
| orderCancelReplace-00010-4.xlsx | orderCancelReplace-00010-4.yaml | 42 | 36 | fix42-10 |
| TAS-00001-4.xlsx | TAS-00001-4.yaml | 7 | 6 | fix42-01 |
| TAS-00001-6.xlsx | TAS-00001-6.yaml | 11 | 10 | fix42-01 |
| TAS-00002-5.xlsx | TAS-00002-5.yaml | 6 | 6 | fix42-02 |
| Hy&BDQry-00010-2.xlsx | Hy&BDQry-00010-2.yaml | 4 | 2 | fix42-10 |

### 转换逻辑

1. **Excel 读取** — `req/rtn` 索引 Sheet 的 CaseNo 列(A列)关联请求与响应，CaseNo=0 为登录回报跳过
2. **YAML 生成** — 标签名用可读名称(`Side: Buy`)，值名用枚举名(`OrdStatus: Rejected`)，`Account: "00001"` 引号保留前导零
3. **多回报** — 一个 CaseNo 对应多个 rtn 行时生成 `expects:` 列表，每一笔有独立的 `match:` 比对字段

### YAML 格式参考

```yaml
name: "orderinsert-00001-1"
steps:
  - step: 1
    request:
      MsgType: D           # 可读标签名
      Side: Buy            # 可读值名
      Account: "00001"     # 引号保前导零
    expect:                # 单回报
      MsgType: 8
      OrdStatus: Rejected
      match: [MsgType, OrdStatus, ClOrdID]
  - step: 2
    request:
      MsgType: D
    expects:               # 多回报
      - MsgType: 8
        ClOrdID: "2"
        match: [MsgType, ClOrdID]
      - MsgType: 8
        ClOrdID: "2"
        match: [MsgType, ClOrdID]
```
